class Dinic:
  def __init__(nodes): adjacency = [[] for _ in range(nodes)]
  def add_edge(fr,to,cap): if cap>1e-12: adj[fr].append([to, cap, len(adj[to])]); adj[to].append([fr,0,len(adj[fr])-1])
  def max_flow(s,t):
    flow=0
    while bfs:
      it=[0]*N
      while True:
        f=dfs(s, INF)
        if f==0: break
        flow += f
    return flow
```

```
def project_simplex_box(y, target):
    y=np.asarray(y,float).ravel()
    n=len(y)
    # binary search lambda
    lo=-1e6, hi=1e6 maybe based y
    for _ in range(80):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=np.clip(y-mid,0,1).sum()
       if s > target: lo=mid else hi=mid
    x=np.clip(y-(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
    # adjust residual
    diff=target-x.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       # distribute to interior
       mask=(x>1e-12)&(x<1-1e-12)
       if mask.any(): x[mask]+=diff/mask.sum()
       else: # adjust one at bounds
          idx=np.argmax((x==1)&(diff>0)??)
    clip
    # final tiny correction
```
Need avoid diff causing bound violation. Use 1e-9 tolerance. If no interior, adjust a bound by diff; if target within [0,n], diff small enough maybe either bound okay? def lse_value_gradient(h, tau):
 D0=T - A[0] # or total - A0
 Cvals = [D0*dx]
 weights etc. Use all vals length m=2n-1 maybe. For memory, create weights arrays wf/wb.

 D = D0 raw, Df, Db.
 wf=0,wb=0 arrays length n
 wf[0]=W0? D0 no directed; for s>0.
 Compute gradient:
   g = -2*W0*h
   # linear: for s in 1..n-1:
      aw=dx*Wf[s]; bw=dx*Wb[s]
      if aw: g[:n-s]+=aw
      if bw: g[s:]+=bw
   # quadratic conv
   for s in 1..n-1:
      wt=dx*(Wf[s]+Wb[s])
      if wt: g[:n-s]-=wt*h[s:]; g[s:]-=wt*h[:n-s]
   value = m + tau* np.log(Wsum)  # in C units
```

def projected_gradient_step(x, n, T, tau, step, momentum=0.0, v=...):
   value,g = lse_value_gradient(x,tau)
   v = momentum*v - step*g
   y = project_simplex_box(x + v, T)
   return y,value,g
```

  best_local=eval(x)
  step=1.0; momentum=0.5; v=zeros
  tau=tau_init
  no_improve=0
  for iter in range(max_iter):
     value,g = lse...
     for mult in [1,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625]:
        st=step*mult
        v = momentum*v - st*g
        y = project(x+v)
        val2,_=lse_value_gradient(y,tau) # expensive; or exact eval
        if val2 < value - 1e-12 or eval(y)<best_local:
            x=y; value=val2; exact=eval; update best_local; no_improve=0; break
     else:
        step*=0.5; if step<1e-5: perturb
     if exact_improvement? ...
     if no_improve > patience: perturb; tau*=0.8; step=1.0; no_improve=0
     if time_left < 30: break
```

Implement function `active_swap_pairs(b, n)`:
```
D0,Df,Db = raw_d(b)
maxD = max(D0, Df.max, Db.max)
active_s = [s where D close max]
pairs=[]
for s in active_s:
  if Df[s]>0:
    idx = np.nonzero((b[0:n-s]==1)&(b[s:n]==0))[0]
    pairs += [(i, i+s) for i in idx]
  if Db[s]>0:
    idx = np.nonzero((b[s:n]==1)&(b[0:n-s]==0))[0] # current i=s..n-1, prev i-s
    pairs += [(i, i-s) for i in idx]
```
This identifies swaps that directly cancel a unit of that overlap. Then choose random from pairs. If many, sample. This may be strong.

We can apply to a small set with line search.

Dinic arrays:
```
class Dinic:
 def __init__(N): self.N=N; self.ad=[[] for _ in range(N)]
 def add_edge(u,v,c):
   if c<=1e-12: return
   self.ad[u].append([v,c,len(self.ad[v])])
   self.ad[v].append([u,0.0,len(self.ad[u])-1])
```

```
if v==t: return f

  self.it[v]=i

  if e[1] > 1e-10 and self.level[v] < self.level[e[0]]:
     ret=dfs(e[0],t,min(f,e[1]))
     if ret>0:
        e[1]-=ret
        self.ad[e[0]][e[2]][1]+=ret
        return ret

def binary_from_float(h, target):
  n=len(h); cnt=int(round(target)); h=int?; if target not int: cnt=floor; need fractional? But we only call for even.

  b=zeros; b[order[:cnt]]=1; return b

- `objective_direct(h)`:

def objective_direct(h):
  n=len(h); dx=2.0/n; c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full'); return float(np.max(c))*dx
```

- `raw_d_values(h)`:
```
def raw_d_values(h):
    n=len(h); h=np.asarray(h,float)
    A=np.correlate(h,h,mode='full')[n-1:]
    total=float(np.sum(h)); cum=np.cumsum(h)
    Df=np.zeros(n); Db=np.zeros(n)
    Df[0]=total - A[0] # actually D0
    if n>1:
      # s=1..n-1
      Df[1:] = cum[n-1:0:-1] - A[1:]
      Db[1:] = total - cum[1:n] - A[1:]
    return Df, Db  # same array Df[1:] and Db[1:]
```
Check P: cum[n-1] for s=1 (sum first n-1); cum[1] for s=n-1. `cum[n-1:0:-1]` length n-1 gives [cum[n-1],...,cum[1]], correct.
Db: total - cum[1] for s=1 (tail from 1), total - cum[n-1] for s=n-1.

raw=max(Df[0], np.max(Df[1:]), np.max(Db[1:])); return raw*2/n
```

Alternatively compute max and softmax over D0 and Df/Db using slices:
```
C0 = D0*dx
Cf = Df[1:]*dx
Cb = Db[1:]*dx
m = max(C0, Cf.max(), Cb.max())
e0 = exp((C0-m)/tau)
ef = exp((Cf-m)/tau)
eb = exp((Cb-m)/tau)
tot = e0+ef.sum()+eb.sum()
w0=e0/tot; wf=ef/tot; wb=eb/tot
```
Then gradient loops over s. This avoids all array.
Value = m + tau*log(tot).
Return value, grad.
Gradient:
```
g = np.zeros(n)
g -= 2.0*w0* h
for s in range(1,n):
    a = wf[s-1]; b=wb[s-1]
    if a !=0: g[:n-s] += dx*a
    if b !=0: g[s:] += dx*b
    wt=a+b
    if wt !=0:
       g[:n-s] -= dx*wt*h[s:]
       g[s:] -= dx*wt*h[:n-s]
```
Note: w arrays are probabilities sum1; scale dx inside. Good.

Potential performance: For each gradient, raw_d_values uses np.correlate O(n^2). Then loop n. SLSQP 200*140 =28k loop, okay. For projected gradient maybe 500.

SLSQP call wrapper:
```
def slsqp_smooth(x0, tau, maxiter=100):
  global _CURRENT_TAU,_CURRENT_N,_CURRENT_TARGET
  n=len(x0); T=n/2.0
  _CURRENT_N=n; _CURRENT_TARGET=T; _CURRENT_TAU=tau
  bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
  res = minimize(slsqp_objective, x0, method='SLSQP', jac=slsqp_jacobian, bounds=bounds,
                constraints=[{'type':'eq','fun':slsqp_con_fun,'jac':slsqp_con_jac}], options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-8,'disp':False})
  x=np.clip(res.x,0,1); x=project_box_sum(x,T)
  return x
```
Need `bounds` list top? no closure.

But SLSQP may call objective before constraint? okay.

Projective gradient:
```
def projected_smooth_optimize(x0, time_limit, tau0, rng, max_iter=5000):
   n=len(x0); T=n/2.0; x=project_box_sum(x0,T)
   remain=time_limit
   step=1.0; momentum=0.5; v=np.zeros(n); tau=tau0; no_improve=0; best_local=objective_direct(x)
   val,g = lse_value_grad(x,tau)
   start=time.time()
   it=0
   while time.time()-start < remain - 5:
      # line search
      improved=False
      for mult in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625,0.03125]:
         st=step*mult
         v=momentum*v - st*g
         y=project_box_sum(x+v,T)
         valy,gy=lse_value_grad(y,tau)
         if valy < val - 1e-12:
            x=y; val=valy; g=gy; improved=True; break
         elif objective_direct(y) < best_local - 1e-12:
            x=y; val=valy; g=gy; best_local=objective_direct(x); best? break
      if not improved:
         step*=0.5
         if step < 1e-4:
            # perturb
            x=perturb(x,0.05,n,rng); val,g=lse...; step=1.0; v=zeros; no_improve=0
         if no_improve>20: ...
         no_improve+=1
      else:
         no_improve=0; if best_local not updated? compute exact maybe every few it.
      # adaptive tau: if no_improve > 10, tau *=0.7?
      if no_improve % 20 ==0:
         tau=max(0.005, tau*0.8); val,g=...
      it+=1
   return x,best_local
```
But objective_direct called many times. We can call exact every 5 iterations or when val improves. Keep local best.

Perturb function:
```
def perturb(x, scale, n, rng):
  x = x + rng.normal(0, scale, n)
  # maybe sparse: mask rng.random(n)<0.3
  return project_box_sum(x,n/2)
```

```
def resample_to_n(h, n):
   m=len(h); if m==n return h
   # treat h as cell centers? positions old (0.5+arange(m))/m*2? new cell centers (0.5+arange(n))/n*2
   x_old=( (np.arange(m)+0.5)/m ) * 2.0
   x_new=( (np.arange(n)+0.5)/n ) * 2.0
   y=np.interp(x_new, x_old, h, left=h[0], right=h[-1])
   return project_box_sum(y, n/2)
```

Rows: 0 cardinality, 1..2n-2 D constraints? Let's map.
D rows: r=1+(s-1) for D_f_s, r=n+s for D_b_s? total 2(n-1). We can add only D constraints; no equality except cardinality.

def active_binary_swaps(h, n, max_pairs=200, rng):
   Df,Db=raw_d_values(h)
   maxD=max(Df[0], Df[1:].max(), Db[1:].max())
   # include lags with D >= maxD - 1e-6
   pairs=[]
   for s in range(1,n):
      if Df[s] >= maxD - 1e-6:
         idx=np.nonzero((h[:n-s]==1)&(h[s:]==0))[0]
         for i in idx: pairs.append((i,i+s))
      if Db[s] >= maxD - 1e-6:
         idx=np.nonzero((h[s:]==1)&(h[:n-s]==0))[0] # current i=s+idx (1), prev idx (0)
         for i in idx: pairs.append((s+i,i))
   if len(pairs)==0: random pairs
   else return rng.choice? We'll select random from pairs each step.
```

```
def _is_feasible(h, target, tol=1e-7): ... (not necessary)

def project_box_sum(y, target):
    y=np.asarray(y,dtype=float).ravel()
    n=y.size; target=float(target)
    if n==0: return y
    if target <= 0: x=0; target=0...
    if target >= n: x=1...
    # initial bracket for lambda: y values min/max
    lo = y.min() - 1.0
    hi = y.max() - 1.0
    # Need sum clip(y - lam). As lam increases, sum decreases.
    for _ in range(80):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       s=np.clip(y-mid,0.0,1.0).sum()
       if s > target:
           lo = mid
       else:
           hi = mid
    lam=0.5*(lo+hi)
    x=np.clip(y-lam,0.0,1.0)
    diff = target - x.sum()
    # Correct diff
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
       mask=(x>1e-9)&(x<1.0-1e-9)
       if np.any(mask):
          x[mask] += diff / int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
       else:
          if diff > 0:
             idx=np.argmin(x) # most room? if x<1
             x[idx] += min(diff, 1.0-x[idx])
          else:
             idx=np.argmax(x)
             x[idx] += max(diff, -x[idx])
    x=np.clip(x,0.0,1.0)
    # If diff still not small due bounds, second pass
    diff=target-x.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       # choose all entries and distribute as much as possible
       for val in np.sort(np.abs(x - 0.5)?): ...
    return x
```
Simpler correction: after projection, diff usually tiny. Do:
```
if abs(diff)>1e-10:
   if diff>0:
      # add to entries not at 1 in increasing (1-x)
      order=np.argsort(1.0 - x)
      rem=diff
      for idx in order:
         space=1.0-x[idx]
         add=min(space, rem)
         x[idx]+=add; rem-=add
         if rem<=1e-12: break
   else:
      order=np.argsort(x)
      rem=-diff
      for idx in order:
         space=x[idx]
         sub=min(space, rem)
         x[idx]-=sub; rem-=sub
         if rem<=1e-12: break
```
This loop in Python but n small. Then final clip and if diff tiny, okay. Feasibility tolerance.

Make binary:
```
def binary_from_float(h, target):
  n=len(h); cnt=int(round(float(target)))
  if cnt<=0: return np.zeros(n,int)
  if cnt>=n: return np.ones(n,int)
  # add tiny deterministic noise to break ties? no rng? use argsort stable
  order=np.argsort(-h, kind='stable')
  b=np.zeros(n,int); b[order[:cnt]]=1
  return b
```
If target .5? For even target int. Use int(round).

Resample:
```
def resample_to_n(h,n):
 h=np.asarray(h,float); m=len(h)
 if m==n: return h.copy()
 if m>0 and n>0:
   if n % m ==0: return np.tile(h, n//m)
 x_old=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m; x_new=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
 y=np.interp(x_new, x_old, h, left=h[0], right=h[-1])
 return y
```

```
def binary_local_search(h_in, time_limit, rng):
  n=len(h_in); T=n//2
  b=binary_from_float(h_in, T).astype(float)
  c=objective_direct(b)
  best=b.copy(); best_c=c
  temp=0.001; min_temp=1e-5; no_imp=0; it=0; start=time.perf_counter()
  patience=200? Maybe adapt.
  while time.perf_counter()-start < time_limit - 2:
     # propose
     if no_imp > 200 and rng.random()<0.1:
        # perturb current: perform 3 random swaps without caring
        for _ in range(3): random swap; update current? We'll just random restart? Maybe later.
     if no_imp > 500:
        # restart from best or random? break? We'll have outer loops in run.
        break
     # choose candidate
     if rng.random()<0.7:
       p,q=active_pair(b,n,rng)
     else:
       p,q=random_pair(b,n,rng)
     if p is None: break
     b[p]=0.0; b[q]=1.0; newc=objective_direct(b)
     accept = newc < c or rng.random() < math.exp(-(newc-c)/temp)
     if accept:
        c=newc
        if newc < best_c:
           best_c=newc; best=b.copy(); no_imp=0
        else: no_imp+=1
     else:
        b[p]=1.0; b[q]=0.0
        no_imp+=1
     temp=max(min_temp, temp*(0.999))
     it+=1
  return best.astype(float), best_c
```

Periodic continuous via SLSQP: Instead of implementing separate projected, use SLSQP with `_periodic` global. Need objective value and jac. Add function:
```
def periodic_value_grad(a, tau):
  m=len(a); h=np.concatenate((a,a)); val,g=lse_value_grad(h,tau); return val, g[:m]+g[m:]
```
Global callbacks:
```
def slsqp_obj_per(a): return periodic_value_grad(a,_PER_TAU)[0]
def slsqp_jac_per(a): return periodic_value_grad(a,_PER_TAU)[1]
def slsqp_con_per(a): return np.array([np.sum(a)-_PER_TARGET])
def slsqp_con_jac_per(a): return np.ones((1,len(a)))
```
Then `slsqp_smooth_periodic(a,tau,maxiter)`:
```
global _PER_TAU,_PER_TARGET
m=len(a); _PER_TAU=tau; _PER_TARGET=m/2
res=minimize(slsqp_obj_per, ... bounds=[(0,1)]*m, constraints=[{... jac slsqp_con_jac_per}], maxiter)
x=project_box_sum(res.x,m/2)
return x
```
But if m odd target not integer; project fractional. Good.

def continuous_local_optimize(x0, time_limit, rng):
  n=len(x0); start=time.perf_counter(); T=n/2; x=project_box_sum(x0,T)
  best=x.copy(); best_c=objective_direct(best)
  # SLSQP from x with tau sequence
  for tau in (0.1,0.04,0.015):
     rem = time_limit - (time.perf_counter()-start)
     if rem < 5: break
     maxit = max(30, int(min(120, rem*2.0))) # if 1/iter ~0.5ms? Actually rem*20? Need safe.
     # For n=140, 120 iter maybe 1s; for 280 maybe 3s. Use maxit=80.
     maxit = int(min(100, max(20, rem*10)))
     x2=slsqp_smooth(x, tau, maxit)
     c=objective_direct(x2); update best
  # Projected gradient on best for remaining 80%? Use all remaining maybe.
  rem = time_limit - elapsed
  if rem >10:
     x3,c3=projected_smooth_optimize(best, rem*0.9, rng, 0.02)
     update
  return best,best_c
```

def projected_smooth_optimize(x0, time_limit, rng, tau=0.03):
  start=time.perf_counter(); n=len; T=n/2; x=project(x0,T); best=x.copy(); bestc=objective_direct(x)
  val,g=lse_value_grad(x,tau); step=1.0; noimp=0; it=0
  while time.perf_counter()-start < time_limit - 1:
     if noimp>=30:
        x=perturb(x,0.05,n,rng); val,g=lse...; step=1.0; noimp=0; tau=max(0.005,tau*0.8)
     improved=False
     for mult in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125):
        y=project_box_sum(x - step*mult*g, T)
        valy,gy=lse_value_grad(y,tau)
        if valy < val - 1e-12:
           x=y; val=valy; g=gy; improved=True; break
     if not improved:
        step *=0.5
        if step < 1e-5:
           x=perturb(...); val,g=...; step=1.0; noimp=0
        noimp +=1
     else:
        noimp=0
        it+=1
        if it % 20 ==0:
          c=objective_direct(x)
          if c<bestc: best,x?
  c=objective_direct(x); update
  return best,bestc
```

Let's write MILP construction carefully to avoid memory large.

```
def solve_milp_binary(n, time_limit):
  try:
    from scipy.optimize import milp, LinearConstraint, Bounds
    from scipy.sparse import coo_matrix
  except Exception:
    return None
  if n % 2 != 0: n -= 1
  if n < 2: return None
  T = n//2
  pair_col = {}
  col = 1+n
  pairs=[]
  for i in range(n-1):
     for j in range(i+1,n):
        pair_col[(i,j)] = col
        pairs.append((i,j,col))
        col += 1
  nvar=col
  nr = 1 + 2*(n-1) + 3*len(pairs)
  rows=[]; cols=[]; data=[]
  def add(r,c,v):
      if v != 0.0:
         rows.append(r); cols.append(c); data.append(v)
  # cardinality row 0
  for i in range(n): add(0,i+1,1.0)
  # D rows
  for s in range(1,n):
     rowf=1+s-1
     rowb=1+n-1+s-1? Let's compute: rowf = s # if 1-index after cardinal? Use 0-based: cardinal row 0; first Df s=1 row 1; Df s row = s. Db s row = 1+(n-1)+(s-1)=n+s-1.
     add(rowf,0,-1.0)
     add(rowb,0,-1.0)
     for i in range(0,n-s):
        j=i+s
        yc=pair_col[(i,j)]
        add(rowf,i+1,1.0); add(rowf,yc,-1.0)
        add(rowb,j+1,1.0); add(rowb,yc,-1.0)
  # mck rows
  row = 1 + 2*(n-1)
  for i,j,yc in pairs:
     add(row,yc,1.0); add(row,i+1,-1.0); row+=1  # y<=zi
     add(row,yc,1.0); add(row,j+1,-1.0); row+=1  # y<=zj
     add(row,yc,-1.0); add(row,i+1,1.0); add(row,j+1,1.0); row+=1 # z_i+z_j-y<=1
  lb = np.full(nr, -np.inf); ub=np.full(nr, np.inf)
  lb[0]=T; ub[0]=T
  # D rows ub 0
  ub[1:1+2*(n-1)] = 0.0
  # mcc ub: first two after D with 0, third 1. Need set per 3.
  base = 1+2*(n-1)
  for k in range(len(pairs)):
     lb is -inf; ub[base+3k]=0; ub[base+3k+1]=0; ub[base+3k+2]=1
  c=np.zeros(nvar); c[0]=1.0
  lbv=np.zeros(nvar); ubv=np.zeros(nvar)
  ubv[0]=float(T)  # M max T maybe? D_f max ≤ T? Since sum≤T. Set T.
  ubv[1:1+n]=1.0
  ubv[1+n:]=1.0
  integ=np.zeros(nvar); integ[1:1+n]=1
  A=coo_matrix((data,(rows,cols)), shape=(nr,nvar))
  cons=LinearConstraint(A, lb=lb, ub=ub)
  bounds=Bounds(lbv, ubv)
  try:
    res=milp(c, constraints=cons, integrality=integ, bounds=bounds, options={'time_limit':int(max(1,time_limit)), 'mip_rel_gap':0.0})
  except Exception:
    return None
  x = getattr(res,'x',None)
  if x is None: return None
  x=np.asarray(x,float)
  z=x[1:1+n]
  # check finite
  if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)): return None
  b=(z>0.5).astype(float)
  # repair count using z fraction
  cnt=int(b.sum())
  if cnt < T:
     need=T-cnt
     zeros=np.nonzero(b==0)[0]
     if len(zeros) < need: # impossible?
        order=np.argsort(-x[1:1+n]) # maybe
        b=...; but shouldn't
     else:
        order=np.argsort(-x[1:1+n])[? zeros]
        # choose zeros with largest z
        frac=x[1:1+n]
        order_zeros=np.argsort(-frac[zeros])
        choose=zeros[order_zeros[:need]]
        b[choose]=1
  elif cnt > T:
     need=cnt-T; ones=np.nonzero(b==1)[0]
     order=np.argsort(x[1:1+n][ones]) # smallest
     choose=ones[order[:need]]; b[choose]=0
  # if still not T, binary_from_float
  if int(b.sum()) != T:
     b=binary_from_float(b, T)
  return b
```

def slsqp_smooth(x0, tau, maxiter=60):
    global _SL_TAU, _SL_N, _SL_TARGET
    x0=np.asarray(x0,float).ravel()
    n=len(x0); T=0.5*n
    _SL_TAU=tau; _SL_N=n; _SL_TARGET=T
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)] for... # can't list comprehension with for in global? It's okay no closure.
    try:
       res=minimize(_sl_obj, x0, method='SLSQP', jac=_sl_jac, bounds=bounds,
                    constraints=[{'type':'eq','fun':_sl_con,'jac':_sl_con_jac}],
                    options={'maxiter':int(maxiter),'ftol':1e-8,'disp':False})
       x=res.x
    except Exception:
       x=x0
    x=np.clip(np.asarray(x,float),0,1)
    return project_box_sum(x,T)
```

Implementation of `lse_value_grad`:
```
def lse_value_grad(h, tau):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    n=h.size; dx=2.0/n; tau=max(1e-6,float(tau)); Df,Db=raw_d_values(h)
    Df=np.maximum(Df,0.0); Db=np.maximum(Db,0.0)
    C0= Df[0]*dx
    if n>1:
       Cf=Df[1:]*dx; Cb=Db[1:]*dx
       m = max(C0, float(np.max(Cf)), float(np.max(Cb)))
       e0 = math.exp((C0-m)/tau)
       ef = np.exp((Cf-m)/tau)
       eb = np.exp((Cb-m)/tau)
       tot = e0 + float(ef.sum()) + float(eb.sum())
       if tot <=0: w0=1.0; wf=zeros; wb=zeros
       else: w0=e0/tot; wf=ef/tot; wb=eb/tot
    else: w0=1; arrays empty
    g = -2.0*w0*h
    for s in range(1,n):
       a=wf[s-1]; b=wb[s-1]
       sl=n-s
       if a != 0.0: g[:sl] += dx*a
       if b != 0.0: g[sl:] += dx*b  # Wait Db indicator i >= s => g[s:], but if sl=n-s, s == sl? For n=4 s=1 sl=3, g[1:]? No, indicator i>=s: i from s to n-1 length n-s = sl. So slice should be g[s:], not g[sl:]. Since s may not equal sl unless n even. I mistakenly. For linear: Df indicator i < n-s => g[:n-s] length sl. Db indicator i >= s => g[s:] length n-s. So `g[s:] += dx*b`.
       wt=a+b
       if wt !=0.0:
          g[:sl] -= dx*wt*h[s:]
          g[s:] -= dx*wt*h[:sl]
    g *= 1.0? Already dx included in contributions, D0 part currently -2w0*h should also be multiplied by dx? Yes gradient of C = dx * gradD. We added dx to linear/conv but not to D0. Need `g = -2.0*w0*dx*h`. Then add dx*linear. So first `g = -2.0*d_x*w0*h`. In conv, use dx*wt. If we g initial multiplied by dx, okay.
    value = m + tau * math.log(tot)
    return float(value), g
```
Check D0: if n=1, D0=0? h[0]=1? sum target0.5 not feasible? ignore.

Gradient Df linear: derivative of P_s = ∑_{i=0}^{n-s-1} h_i, indicator i <= n-s-1 => slice `:n-s` yes `:sl`. Good. Db: Q_s = ∑_{i=s}^{n-1} h_i, indicator i>=s => slice `s:`. Conv term: A_s=∑_{i=0}^{n-s-1}h_i h_{i+s}. Derivative - h_{i+s} for h_i (slice :sl) and -h_{i-s} for h_{i+s} (slice s:). Good.

Value units: m in C units (dx). Good.

`raw_d_values`: Df[0]=total - A[0] (A0 sum squares). For binary total=T, A0=T ->0. For fractional total N/2, A0 maybe. C0 = D0*dx. Good.

`projected_smooth_optimize`: In line search, if `val - step*mult*g`, g units C; x units [0,1]. Step maybe dimensionless. If g ~0.05, step 1 -> change 0.05. Good. If g zero, no improvement; perturb.

`slsqp_smooth`: SLSQP minimize smooth value; but smooth value can have local flat. Since equality constraint sum, it may take small steps. Provide gradient. Good.

Potential issue: `SLSQP` with many variables and equality may be slow to satisfy sum if x0 close. Fine.

Now, `run` details and time management. Let's pseudo-code with dynamic allocations:
```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1300,**kwargs):
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    left = deadline - t0
    # parent
    parent = None
    if 'initial_h_values' in globals():
       parent = np.asarray(globals()['initial_h_values'], dtype=float)
       if parent.size:
          parent = project_box_sum(parent, len(parent)/2)
    # no parent fallback
    n_fallback = 70
    if parent is None:
       # create structured candidates
       h = triangular(n_fallback,...); project
       parent = h
    best_h = parent.copy()
    best_c = objective_direct(best_h)
    best_n = len(best_h)
    # If parent not bound <1, maybe evaluate other candidates initial
    for kind in range(5):
       h = structured_candidate(kind, len(parent), rng)
       c=objective_direct(h)
       if c<best_c: update
    # If parent length odd? make even? For n=70 even.
```

def structured_candidate(kind, n, rng): ...
```

```
def resample_to_n(h,n):
 h = np.asarray(h,float); m=h.size
 if m==n: return h.copy()
 if m % n == 0:
    h2 = np.mean(h.reshape(n, m//n), axis=1); return h2
 if n % m == 0:
    return np.tile(h, n//m)
 # interp
 x_old=...; x_new=...
 return np.interp(...)
```

Now, `binary_local_search` active pool:
Implement `build_active_pool` top-level. It needs `raw_d_values` which returns Df,Db raw. It may be called many times, so threshold. It should not use nested.
```
def build_active_pool(b, n, rng, threshold=0.6, max_pairs=2000):
  b=np.asarray(b,float)
  if n<2: return []
  Df,Db=raw_d_values(b)
  maxD = float(Df[0])
  if n>1:
    maxD = max(maxD, float(np.max(Df[1:])), float(np.max(Db[1:])))
  pool=[]
  # if maxD <=1e-9 return []
  for s in range(1,n):
    if maxD > 0 and Df[s] > max(threshold*maxD, 1e-8):
       mask = (b[:n-s] > 0.5) & (b[s:] < 0.5)
       idx = np.nonzero(mask)[0]
       if idx.size:
          if idx.size > 50:
             pick = rng.choice(idx, size=50, replace=False)
          else: pick=idx
          for i in pick:
             pool.append((int(i), int(i+s)))
    if maxD > 0 and Db[s] > max(threshold*maxD, 1e-8):
       mask = (b[s:] > 0.5) & (b[:n-s] < 0.5)
       idx = np.nonzero(mask)[0]
       if idx.size:
          if idx.size > 50: pick = rng.choice(idx, size=50, replace=False)
          else: pick=idx
          for i in pick:
             pool.append((int(s+i), int(i)))
  if len(pool) > max_pairs:
     sel = rng.choice(len(pool), size=max_pairs, replace=False)
     pool = [pool[i] for i in sel]
  return pool
```
For Db, D_b pair: current i=s+i0 = 1, prev i0 =0, swap p=current (1) to q=prev (0). Good.

In `binary_local_search`, maintain `pool=[]; pool_iter=0`. Every `rebuild_every=20` iterations or when pool empty. But if active no pairs, rebuild. When accept, maybe re-evaluate? The pool remains. To avoid iterating all accepted newc and direct objective using full correlate, okay. Pseudocode:
```
def binary_local_search(h_in, time_limit, rng):
  n=len(h_in); T=n//2
  if T<0 or 2*T != n: ...? If n odd, use target n/2 not int impossible binary; skip or use T=floor.
  b=binary_from_float(h_in,T).astype(float)
  c=objective_direct(b)
  best=b.copy(); best_c=c
  start=time.perf_counter(); temp=0.0008; min_temp=1e-5; noimp=0; pool=[]; rebuild=0
  while time.perf_counter()-start < time_limit - 1 and time_left global? It uses its own time_limit.
     if rebuild>=20 or not pool:
        pool=build_active_pool(b,n,rng,0.65)
        rebuild=0
     rebuild+=1
     if pool and rng.random()<0.7:
        p,q=pool[rng.integers(0,len(pool))]
     else:
        pair=random_pair_binary(b,n,rng)
        if pair is None: break
        p,q=pair
     b[p]=0.0; b[q]=1.0
     newc=objective_direct(b)
     if newc < c or (newc-c)/temp < 700 and rng.random() < math.exp(-(newc-c)/temp):
        c=newc
        if newc < best_c:
           best_c=newc; best=b.copy(); noimp=0
        else: noimp+=1
     else:
        b[p]=1.0; b[q]=0.0
        noimp+=1
     temp=max(min_temp, temp*(0.9995))
     if noimp > 1000:
        # perform 5 random swaps to perturb then reset noimp; c may increase
        for _ in range(5):
          pair=random_pair_binary(b,n,rng)
          if pair: b[p]=0;b[q]=1
        c=objective_direct(b)
        noimp=0; pool=[]
     if noimp > 2000:
        break
  return best.astype(float), float(best_c)
```

delta = newc - c

def continuous_local_optimize(x0, time_limit, rng):

     maxit = min(80, max(20, int((time_limit-elapsed)*0.2)))? Need if 0.2/s? Let's just 60.
     x = slsqp_smooth(best, tau, maxiter=60)

def periodic_value_grad(a, tau):

```
def structured_candidate(kind, n, rng):
  if n <=0: return np.array([])
  x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)
  if kind==0:
    h=np.clip(1.0-np.abs(x-1.0),0,1)
  elif kind==1:
    h=0.5+0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*x)
  elif kind==2:
    h=(np.abs(x-1.0) <= 0.5).astype(float)
  elif kind==3:
    h=((x <= 0.25) | (x >= 1.75)).astype(float)
  elif kind==4:
    h=binary_from_float(rng.random(n), n/2.0).astype(float)
  elif kind==5:
    h=np.exp(-((x-1.0)**2)/(2*0.25**2)); h = h/np.sum(h)*n/2.0? Better use project
    # set project later
    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
  elif kind==6:
    # tent with plateau
    y=np.abs(x-1.0)
    h=np.clip(1.0 - np.maximum(0, y-0.2)/0.3,0,1)
  else:
    h=rng.random(n)
  return project_box_sum(h, n/2.0)
```
For kind5, no mass, projection scales.

`project_box_sum` distribution loops:
```
def project_box_sum(y, target):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float).ravel()
    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    n = y.size
    target = float(target)
    if n == 0:
        return y
    if target <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if target >= n:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    lo = float(np.min(y)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(y)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(80):
        mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
        s = float(np.clip(y-mid,0.0,1.0).sum())
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    x = np.clip(y - 0.5*(lo+hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(x.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
       if diff > 0:
          order = np.argsort(1.0 - x)
          rem = diff
          for idx in order:
             space = 1.0 - float(x[idx])
             if space <= 0: continue
             add = min(space, rem)
             x[idx] += add
             rem -= add
             if rem <= 1e-12: break
       else:
          order = np.argsort(x)
          rem = -diff
          for idx in order:
             space = float(x[idx])
             if space <=0: continue
             sub = min(space, rem)
             x[idx] -= sub
             rem -= sub
             if rem <=1e-12: break
    x = np.clip(x,0,1)
    return x
```
If diff unfixable due bounds, but target within [0,n], should. If tiny residual remains, not adjust. Good.

`objective_direct`:
```
def objective_direct(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    if h.size==0: return 1.0
    h=np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    dx=2.0/len(h)
    c=np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
    val=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not math.isfinite(val): return 1.0
    return max(0.0,val)*dx
```
If h length n. Good.

def perturb(x, scale, n, rng):

```python
import time
import math
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
except Exception:
    minimize = None

_SL_TAU = 1.0
_SL_N = 0
_SL_TARGET = 0.0

_PER_TAU = 1.0
_PER_TARGET = 0.0


def sparse_add(rows, cols, data, r, c, v):
    if v != 0.0:
        rows.append(int(r))
        cols.append(int(c))
        data.append(float(v))


def project_box_sum(y, target):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float).ravel()
    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    n = y.size
    target = float(target)

    if n == 0:
        return y
    if target <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if target >= n:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=float)

    lo = float(np.min(y)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(y)) + 1.0

    for _ in range(80):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.clip(y - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum())
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid

    x = np.clip(y - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)

    diff = target - float(x.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        if diff > 0.0:
            order = np.argsort(1.0 - x)
            rem = diff
            for idx in order:
                space = 1.0 - float(x[idx])
                if space <= 0.0:
                    continue
                add = min(space, rem)
                x[idx] += add
                rem -= add
                if rem <= 1e-12:
                    break
        else:
            order = np.argsort(x)
            rem = -diff
            for idx in order:
                space = float(x[idx])
                if space <= 0.0:
                    continue
                sub = min(space, rem)
                x[idx] -= sub
                rem -= sub
                if rem <= 1e-12:
                    break

    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    return x


def binary_from_float(h, target):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = h.size
    cnt = int(round(float(target)))
    cnt = max(0, min(n, cnt))

    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if cnt <= 0:
        return b
    if cnt >= n:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=float)

    order = np.argsort(-h, kind="stable")
    b[order[:cnt]] = 1.0
    return b


def resample_to_n(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel()
    m = h.size

    if m == n:
        return h.copy()
    if m == 0 or n == 0:
        return np.zeros(max(0, n), dtype=float)

    if m % n == 0:
        h2 = h.reshape(n, m // n).mean(axis=1)
        return h2
    if n % m == 0:
        return np.tile(h, n // m)

    x_old = (np.arange(m, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(m)
    x_new = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(n)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h, left=float(h[0]), right=float(h[-1]))
    return y


def objective_direct(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if h.size == 0:
        return 1.0
    h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    n = h.size
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0

    val = float(np.max(c))
    if not math.isfinite(val):
        return 1.0
    return max(0.0, val) * dx


def raw_d_values(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel()
    h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    n = h.size

    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0), np.zeros(0)

    A = np.correlate(h, h, mode="full")[n - 1:]
    total = float(h.sum())
    cum = np.cumsum(h)

    Df = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    Db = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    Df[0] = total - float(A[0])

    if n > 1:
        Df[1:] = cum[n - 1:0:-1] - A[1:]
        Db[1:] = total - cum[1:n] - A[1:]

    return Df, Db


def c5_from_raw(h, Df, Db):
    n = np.asarray(h).size
    if n == 0:
        return 1.0
    Dx = np.maximum(Df, 0.0)
    Dy = np.maximum(Db, 0.0)
    val = max(float(np.max(Dx)), float(np.max(Dy)))
    return max(0.0, val) * (2.0 / float(n))


def lse_value_grad(h, tau):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel()
    h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    n = h.size

    if n == 0:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(0)

    tau = max(1e-8, float(tau))
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)

    Df, Db = raw_d_values(h)
    Df = np.maximum(Df, 0.0)
    Db = np.maximum(Db, 0.0)

    C0 = float(Df[0]) * dx

    if n > 1:
        Cf = Df[1:] * dx
        Cb = Db[1:] * dx
        m = max(C0, float(np.max(Cf)), float(np.max(Cb)))

        e0 = math.exp((C0 - m) / tau)
        ef = np.exp((Cf - m) / tau)
        eb = np.exp((Cb - m) / tau)

        tot = e0 + float(ef.sum()) + float(eb.sum())
        if tot <= 0.0:
            w0 = 1.0
            wf = np.zeros(n - 1)
            wb = np.zeros(n - 1)
        else:
            w0 = e0 / tot
            wf = ef / tot
            wb = eb / tot

        value = m + tau * math.log(tot)
    else:
        w0 = 1.0
        wf = np.zeros(0)
        wb = np.zeros(0)
        value = C0

    g = -2.0 * dx * w0 * h

    for s in range(1, n):
        sl = n - s
        a = float(wf[s - 1])
        b = float(wb[s - 1])

        if a != 0.0:
            g[:sl] += dx * a
        if b != 0.0:
            g[s:] += dx * b

        wt = a + b
        if wt != 0.0:
            g[:sl] -= dx * wt * h[s:]
            g[s:] -= dx * wt * h[:sl]

    g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    if not math.isfinite(value):
        value = 0.0

    return float(value), g


def _sl_objective(x):
    val, _ = lse_value_grad(x, _SL_TAU)
    return val


def _sl_jacobian(x):
    _, g = lse_value_grad(x, _SL_TAU)
    return g


def _sl_constraint(x):
    return np.array([float(np.sum(x)) - _SL_TARGET], dtype=float)


def _sl_constraint_jac(x):
    return np.ones((1, len(x)), dtype=float)


def slsqp_smooth(x0, tau, maxiter=60):
    x0 = np.asarray(x0, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = x0.size
    T = 0.5 * float(n)

    if minimize is None:
        return project_box_sum(x0, T)

    global _SL_TAU, _SL_N, _SL_TARGET
    _SL_TAU = max(1e-8, float(tau))
    _SL_N = n
    _SL_TARGET = T

    x0 = project_box_sum(x0, T)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    constraints = [
        {
            "type": "eq",
            "fun": _sl_constraint,
            "jac": _sl_constraint_jac,
        }
    ]

    try:
        res = minimize(
            _sl_objective,
            x0,
            method="SLSQP",
            jac=_sl_jacobian,
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=constraints,
            options={"maxiter": int(max(1, maxiter)), "ftol": 1e-8, "disp": False},
        )
        x = getattr(res, "x", None)
        if x is None:
            x = x0
    except Exception:
        x = x0

    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    return project_box_sum(x, T)


def perturb(x, scale, rng):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return x
    mask = rng.random(n) < 0.35
    noise = rng.normal(0.0, float(scale), n)
    y = x.copy()
    y[mask] += noise[mask]
    return project_box_sum(y, 0.5 * float(n))


def projected_smooth_optimize(x0, time_limit, rng, tau=0.03):
    start = time.perf_counter()
    x0 = np.asarray(x0, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = x0.size
    if n == 0:
        return x0, 1.0

    T = 0.5 * float(n)
    x = project_box_sum(x0, T)
    best = x.copy()
    best_c = objective_direct(best)

    step = 1.0
    noimp = 0
    it = 0
    tau = max(1e-5, float(tau))

    val, g = lse_value_grad(x, tau)
    if not math.isfinite(val):
        x = perturb(x, 0.04, rng)
        val, g = lse_value_grad(x, tau)

    while time.perf_counter() - start < max(0.0, float(time_limit)) - 1.0:
        if noimp >= 30:
            x = perturb(x, 0.04, rng)
            val, g = lse_value_grad(x, tau)
            step = 1.0
            tau = max(0.003, tau * 0.85)
            noimp = 0

        improved = False
        for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125):
            y = project_box_sum(x - step * mult * g, T)
            valy, gy = lse_value_grad(y, tau)
            if math.isfinite(valy) and valy < val - 1e-12:
                x = y
                val = valy
                g = gy
                improved = True
                break

        if not improved:
            step *= 0.5
            noimp += 1
            if step < 1e-5:
                x = perturb(x, 0.05, rng)
                val, g = lse_value_grad(x, tau)
                step = 1.0
                noimp = 0
        else:
            noimp = 0
            it += 1
            if it % 25 == 0:
                c = objective_direct(x)
                if c < best_c:
                    best_c = c
                    best = x.copy()

    c = objective_direct(x)
    if c < best_c:
        best = x.copy()
        best_c = c

    return best, float(best_c)


def continuous_local_optimize(x0, time_limit, rng):
    start = time.perf_counter()
    x0 = np.asarray(x0, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = x0.size
    if n == 0:
        return x0, 1.0

    x = project_box_sum(x0, 0.5 * float(n))
    best = x.copy()
    best_c = objective_direct(best)

    for tau in (0.10, 0.04, 0.015):
        elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start
        if float(time_limit) - elapsed < 4.0:
            break
        x = slsqp_smooth(best, tau, maxiter=70)
        c = objective_direct(x)
        if c < best_c:
            best_c = c
            best = x.copy()

    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start
    rem = max(0.0, float(time_limit) - elapsed - 2.0)
    if rem > 10.0:
        x2, c2 = projected_smooth_optimize(best, rem, rng, tau=0.02)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best = x2
            best_c = c2

    return best, float(best_c)


def periodic_value_grad(a, tau):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    m = a.size
    if m == 0:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(0)
    h = np.concatenate((a, a))
    val, g = lse_value_grad(h, tau)
    return val, g[:m] + g[m:]


def _per_objective(a):
    val, _ = periodic_value_grad(a, _PER_TAU)
    return val


def _per_jacobian(a):
    _, g = periodic_value_grad(a, _PER_TAU)
    return g


def _per_constraint(a):
    return np.array([float(np.sum(a)) - _PER_TARGET], dtype=float)


def _per_constraint_jac(a):
    return np.ones((1, len(a)), dtype=float)


def slsqp_smooth_periodic(a0, tau, maxiter=60):
    a0 = np.asarray(a0, dtype=float).ravel()
    m = a0.size
    T = 0.5 * float(m)

    if minimize is None:
        return project_box_sum(a0, T)

    global _PER_TAU, _PER_TARGET
    _PER_TAU = max(1e-8, float(tau))
    _PER_TARGET = T

    a0 = project_box_sum(a0, T)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * m
    constraints = [
        {
            "type": "eq",
            "fun": _per_constraint,
            "jac": _per_constraint_jac,
        }
    ]

    try:
        res = minimize(
            _per_objective,
            a0,
            method="SLSQP",
            jac=_per_jacobian,
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=constraints,
            options={"maxiter": int(max(1, maxiter)), "ftol": 1e-8, "disp": False},
        )
        a = getattr(res, "x", None)
        if a is None:
            a = a0
    except Exception:
        a = a0

    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1.0)
    return project_box_sum(a, T)


def periodic_projected_optimize(a0, time_limit, rng, tau=0.03):
    start = time.perf_counter()
    a0 = np.asarray(a0, dtype=float).ravel()
    m = a0.size
    if m == 0:
        return a0, 1.0

    T = 0.5 * float(m)
    a = project_box_sum(a0, T)
    full = np.concatenate((a, a))
    best = a.copy()
    best_c = objective_direct(full)

    step = 1.0
    noimp = 0
    it = 0
    tau = max(1e-5, float(tau))

    val, g = periodic_value_grad(a, tau)
    if not math.isfinite(val):
        a = project_box_sum(a + rng.normal(0.0, 0.04, m), T)
        val, g = periodic_value_grad(a, tau)

    while time.perf_counter() - start < max(0.0, float(time_limit)) - 1.0:
        if noimp >= 30:
            a = project_box_sum(a + rng.normal(0.0, 0.04, m), T)
            val, g = periodic_value_grad(a, tau)
            step = 1.0
            tau = max(0.003, tau * 0.85)
            noimp = 0

        improved = False
        for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125):
            y = project_box_sum(a - step * mult * g, T)
            valy, gy = periodic_value_grad(y, tau)
            if math.isfinite(valy) and valy < val - 1e-12:
                a = y
                val = valy
                g = gy
                improved = True
                break

        if not improved:
            step *= 0.5
            noimp += 1
            if step < 1e-5:
                a = project_box_sum(a + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, m), T)
                val, g = periodic_value_grad(a, tau)
                step = 1.0
                noimp = 0
        else:
            noimp = 0
            it += 1
            if it % 25 == 0:
                full = np.concatenate((a, a))
                c = objective_direct(full)
                if c < best_c:
                    best_c = c
                    best = a.copy()

    full = np.concatenate((a, a))
    c = objective_direct(full)
    if c < best_c:
        best = a.copy()
        best_c = c

    return best, float(best_c)


def periodic_continuous_optimize(a0, time_limit, rng):
    start = time.perf_counter()
    a0 = np.asarray(a0, dtype=float).ravel()
    m = a0.size
    if m == 0:
        return a0, 1.0

    a = project_box_sum(a0, 0.5 * float(m))
    full = np.concatenate((a, a))
    best = a.copy()
    best_c = objective_direct(full)

    for tau in (0.10, 0.04, 0.015):
        elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start
        if float(time_limit) - elapsed < 4.0:
            break
        a = slsqp_smooth_periodic(best, tau, maxiter=70)
        full = np.concatenate((a, a))
        c = objective_direct(full)
        if c < best_c:
            best = a.copy()
            best_c = c

    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start
    rem = max(0.0, float(time_limit) - elapsed - 2.0)
    if rem > 10.0:
        a2, c2 = periodic_projected_optimize(best, rem, rng, tau=0.02)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best = a2
            best_c = c2

    return best, float(best_c)


def structured_candidate(kind, n, rng):
    n = int(max(0, n))
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=float)

    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))

    if kind == 0:
        h = np.clip(1.0 - np.abs(x - 1.0), 0.0, 1.0)
    elif kind == 1:
        h = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * x)
    elif kind == 2:
        h = (np.abs(x - 1.0) <= 0.5).astype(float)
    elif kind == 3:
        h = ((x <= 0.25) | (x >= 1.75)).astype(float)
    elif kind == 4:
        h = binary_from_float(rng.random(n), 0.5 * float(n))
    elif kind == 5:
        h = np.exp(-((x - 1.0) ** 2) / (2.0 * 0.22 ** 2))
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    elif kind == 6:
        y = np.abs(x - 1.0)
        h = np.clip(1.0 - np.maximum(0.0, y - 0.2) / 0.3, 0.0, 1.0)
    else:
        h = rng.random(n)

    return project_box_sum(h, 0.5 * float(n))


def random_pair_binary(b, n, rng):
    ones = np.nonzero(b > 0.5)[0]
    zeros = np.nonzero(b < 0.5)[0]
    if ones.size == 0 or zeros.size == 0:
        return None
    pi = int(rng.integers(0, ones.size))
    qi = int(rng.integers(0, zeros.size))
    return int(ones[pi]), int(zeros[qi])


def build_active_pool(b, n, rng, threshold=0.65, max_pairs=2000):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = b.size
    if n < 2:
        return []

    Df, Db = raw_d_values(b)
    maxD = float(Df[0])
    if n > 1:
        maxD = max(maxD, float(np.max(Df[1:])), float(np.max(Db[1:])))

    pool = []
    if maxD <= 1e-9:
        return pool

    for s in range(1, n):
        sl = n - s

        if Df[s] > max(threshold * maxD, 1e-8):
            mask = (b[:sl] > 0.5) & (b[s:] < 0.5)
            idx = np.nonzero(mask)[0]
            if idx.size:
                if idx.size > 50:
                    pick = rng.choice(idx, size=50, replace=False)
                else:
                    pick = idx
                for ii in pick:
                    pool.append((int(ii), int(ii + s)))

        if Db[s] > max(threshold * maxD, 1e-8):
            mask = (b[s:] > 0.5) & (b[:sl] < 0.5)
            idx = np.nonzero(mask)[0]
            if idx.size:
                if idx.size > 50:
                    pick = rng.choice(idx, size=50, replace=False)
                else:
                    pick = idx
                for ii in pick:
                    pool.append((int(ii + s), int(ii)))

    if len(pool) > max_pairs:
        sel = rng.choice(len(pool), size=max_pairs, replace=False)
        pool = [pool[int(i)] for i in sel]

    return pool


def binary_local_search(h_in, time_limit, rng):
    h_in = np.asarray(h_in, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = h_in.size
    if n == 0 or n % 2 != 0:
        return h_in, objective_direct(h_in)

    T = 0.5 * float(n)
    b = binary_from_float(h_in, T).astype(float)
    c = objective_direct(b)

    best = b.copy()
    best_c = c

    start = time.perf_counter()
    temp = 0.0010
    min_temp = 1e-5
    noimp = 0
    pool = []
    rebuild = 0

    while time.perf_counter() - start < max(0.0, float(time_limit)) - 1.0:
        if rebuild >= 20 or len(pool) == 0:
            pool = build_active_pool(b, n, rng, threshold=0.65, max_pairs=2000)
            rebuild = 0
        rebuild += 1

        if len(pool) > 0 and rng.random() < 0.70:
            idx = int(rng.integers(0, len(pool)))
            p, q = pool[idx]
        else:
            pr = random_pair_binary(b, n, rng)
            if pr is None:
                break
            p, q = pr

        b[p] = 0.0
        b[q] = 1.0
        newc = objective_direct(b)

        delta = newc - c
        if delta <= 1e-12:
            accept = True
        else:
            z = delta / temp
            accept = (z < 700.0) and (rng.random() < math.exp(-z))

        if accept:
            c = newc
            if c < best_c:
                best_c = c
                best = b.copy()
            noimp += 1
        else:
            b[p] = 1.0
            b[q] = 0.0
            noimp += 1

        temp = max(min_temp, temp * 0.9995)

        if noimp > 1200:
            for _ in range(5):
                pr = random_pair_binary(b, n, rng)
                if pr is not None:
                    p, q = pr
                    b[p] = 0.0
                    b[q] = 1.0
            c = objective_direct(b)
            noimp = 0
            pool = []
            rebuild = 0

        if noimp > 3000:
            break

    return best, float(best_c)


def solve_milp_binary(n, time_limit):
    try:
        from scipy.optimize import milp, LinearConstraint, Bounds
        from scipy.sparse import coo_matrix
    except Exception:
        return None

    if n is None:
        return None
    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 != 0:
        n -= 1
    if n < 2:
        return None

    T = n // 2

    pair_col = {}
    pairs = []
    col = 1 + n
    for i in range(n - 1):
        for j in range(i + 1, n):
            pair_col[(i, j)] = col
            pairs.append((i, j, col))
            col += 1

    nvars = col
    nr = 1 + 2 * (n - 1) + 3 * len(pairs)

    rows = []
    cols = []
    data = []

    for i in range(n):
        sparse_add(rows, cols, data, 0, i + 1, 1.0)

    for s in range(1, n):
        rowf = s
        rowb = n + s - 1
        sparse_add(rows, cols, data, rowf, 0, -1.0)
        sparse_add(rows, cols, data, rowb, 0, -1.0)

        for i in range(0, n - s):
            j = i + s
            yc = pair_col[(i, j)]
            sparse_add(rows, cols, data, rowf, i + 1, 1.0)
            sparse_add(rows, cols, data, rowf, yc, -1.0)
            sparse_add(rows, cols, data, rowb, j + 1, 1.0)
            sparse_add(rows, cols, data, rowb, yc, -1.0)

    row = 1 + 2 * (n - 1)
    for i, j, yc in pairs:
        sparse_add(rows, cols, data, row, yc, 1.0)
        sparse_add(rows, cols, data, row, i + 1, -1.0)
        row += 1

        sparse_add(rows, cols, data, row, yc, 1.0)
        sparse_add(rows, cols, data, row, j + 1, -1.0)
        row += 1

        sparse_add(rows, cols, data, row, yc, -1.0)
        sparse_add(rows, cols, data, row, i + 1, 1.0)
        sparse_add(rows, cols, data, row, j + 1, 1.0)
        row += 1

    lb = np.full(nr, -np.inf, dtype=float)
    ub = np.full(nr, np.inf, dtype=float)

    lb[0] = float(T)
    ub[0] = float(T)

    ub[1:1 + 2 * (n - 1)] = 0.0

    base = 1 + 2 * (n - 1)
    for k in range(len(pairs)):
        ub[base + 3 * k] = 0.0
        ub[base + 3 * k + 1] = 0.0
        ub[base + 3 * k + 2] = 1.0

    c = np.zeros(nvars, dtype=float)
    c[0] = 1.0

    lbv = np.zeros(nvars, dtype=float)
    ubv = np.zeros(nvars, dtype=float)
    ubv[0] = float(T)
    ubv[1:1 + n] = 1.0
    ubv[1 + n:] = 1.0

    integrality = np.zeros(nvars, dtype=int)
    integrality[1:1 + n] = 1

    A = coo_matrix((data, (rows, cols)), shape=(nr, nvars))
    cons = LinearConstraint(A, lb=lb, ub=ub)
    bounds = Bounds(lbv, ubv)

    try:
        res = milp(
            c=c,
            constraints=cons,
            integrality=integrality,
            bounds=bounds,
            options={
                "time_limit": int(max(1, min(120, float(time_limit)))),
                "mip_rel_gap": 0.0,
            },
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    x = getattr(res, "x", None)
    if x is None:
        return None

    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return None

    z = x[1:1 + n]
    b = (z > 0.5).astype(float)

    cnt = int(b.sum())
    if cnt < T:
        need = T - cnt
        zeros = np.nonzero(b == 0.0)[0]
        if zeros.size >= need:
            order = np.argsort(-z[zeros])
            choose = zeros[order[:need]]
            b[choose] = 1.0
    elif cnt > T:
        need = cnt - T
        ones = np.nonzero(b == 1.0)[0]
        if ones.size >= need:
            order = np.argsort(z[ones])
            choose = ones[order[:need]]
            b[choose] = 0.0

    if int(b.sum()) != T:
        b = binary_from_float(b, float(T))

    return b


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = None
    if "initial_h_values" in globals():
        try:
            p = np.asarray(globals()["initial_h_values"], dtype=float).ravel()
            if p.size > 0:
                parent = project_box_sum(p, 0.5 * float(p.size))
        except Exception:
            parent = None

    if parent is None:
        parent = structured_candidate(0, 70, rng)

    best_h = parent.copy()
    best_c = objective_direct(best_h)
    best_n = int(best_h.size)

    for k in range(7):
        remaining = deadline - time.perf_counter()
        if remaining < 5.0:
            break
        h0 = structured_candidate(k, best_n, rng)
        c0 = objective_direct(h0)
        if c0 < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_c = c0
            best_h = h0.copy()
            best_n = int(h0.size)

    milp_h = None
    remaining = deadline - time.perf_counter()
    if remaining > 60.0:
        n_m = best_n if (best_n % 2 == 0 and 2 <= best_n <= 80) else 70
        t_milp = min(70.0, max(15.0, 0.07 * float(budget_s)))
        if t_milp > remaining - 30.0:
            t_milp = max(10.0, remaining - 30.0)
        try:
            milp_h = solve_milp_binary(n_m, t_milp)
        except Exception:
            milp_h = None
        if milp_h is not None:
            milp_h = np.asarray(milp_h, dtype=float)
            c0 = objective_direct(milp_h)
            if c0 < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c = c0
                best_h = milp_h.copy()
                best_n = int(milp_h.size)

    remaining = deadline - time.perf_counter()
    if remaining > 50.0 and best_n % 2 == 0:
        t_bin = min(90.0, max(15.0, 0.07 * float(budget_s)))
        if t_bin > remaining - 25.0:
            t_bin = max(10.0, remaining - 25.0)
        try:
            hb, cb = binary_local_search(parent, t_bin, rng)
            if cb < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c = cb
                best_h = hb.copy()
                best_n = int(hb.size)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if milp_h is not None:
        remaining = deadline - time.perf_counter()
        if remaining > 30.0:
            t_bin2 = min(45.0, max(10.0, 0.04 * float(budget_s)))
            if t_bin2 > remaining - 20.0:
                t_bin2 = max(8.0, remaining - 20.0)
            try:
                hb, cb = binary_local_search(milp_h, t_bin2, rng)
                if cb < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best_c = cb
                    best_h = hb.copy()
                    best_n = int(hb.size)
            except Exception:
                pass

    remaining = deadline - time.perf_counter()
    if remaining > 60.0:
        t_c = min(120.0, max(20.0, 0.09 * float(budget_s)))
        if t_c > remaining - 25.0:
            t_c = max(15.0, remaining - 25.0)
        try:
            hc, cc = continuous_local_optimize(best_h, t_c, rng)
            if cc < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c = cc
                best_h = hc.copy()
                best_n = int(hc.size)
        except Exception:
            pass

    remaining = deadline - time.perf_counter()
    if remaining > 150.0:
        if best_n == 140:
            h140 = best_h.copy()
        elif parent is not None and parent.size == 70:
            h140 = np.tile(parent, 2)
        else:
            h140 = resample_to_n(best_h, 140)

        h140 = project_box_sum(h140, 70.0)
        t_c140 = min(180.0, max(30.0, 0.14 * float(budget_s)))
        if t_c140 > remaining - 60.0:
            t_c140 = max(20.0, remaining - 60.0)

        try:
            h140, c140 = continuous_local_optimize(h140, t_c140, rng)
            if c140 < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c = c140
                best_h = h140.copy()
                best_n = int(h140.size)
        except Exception:
            pass

        remaining = deadline - time.perf_counter()
        if remaining > 60.0:
            t_b140 = min(60.0, max(15.0, 0.05 * float(budget_s)))
            if t_b140 > remaining - 30.0:
                t_b140 = max(10.0, remaining - 30.0)
            try:
                hb, cb = binary_local_search(h140, t_b140, rng)
                if cb < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best_c = cb
                    best_h = hb.copy()
                    best_n = int(hb.size)
            except Exception:
                pass

    remaining = deadline - time.perf_counter()
    if remaining > 120.0:
        m = 70
        if best_n == 140:
            a0 = 0.5 * (best_h[:m] + best_h[m:])
        elif parent is not None and parent.size == 70:
            a0 = parent.copy()
        else:
            a0 = resample_to_n(parent if parent is not None else best_h, m)

        a0 = project_box_sum(a0, 0.5 * float(m))
        t_per = min(150.0, max(25.0, 0.12 * float(budget_s)))
        if t_per > remaining - 40.0:
            t_per = max(20.0, remaining - 40.0)

        try:
            ap, cp = periodic_continuous_optimize(a0, t_per, rng)
            hper = np.concatenate((ap, ap))
            hper = project_box_sum(hper, float(m))
            cper = objective_direct(hper)
            if cper < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c = cper
                best_h = hper.copy()
                best_n = int(hper.size)
        except Exception:
            pass

    remaining = deadline - time.perf_counter()
    if remaining > 40.0:
        tfinal = max(10.0, remaining - 5.0)
        try:
            hf, cf = continuous_local_optimize(best_h, tfinal, rng)
            if cf < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c = cf
                best_h = hf.copy()
                best_n = int(hf.size)
        except Exception:
            pass

        remaining = deadline - time.perf_counter()
        if best_n % 2 == 0 and best_n >= 2 and remaining > 60.0:
            m = best_n // 2
            a0 = 0.5 * (best_h[:m] + best_h[m:])
            a0 = project_box_sum(a0, 0.5 * float(m))
            try:
                ap, cp = periodic_continuous_optimize(a0, min(80.0, remaining - 10.0), rng)
                hper = np.concatenate((ap, ap))
                hper = project_box_sum(hper, float(m))
                cper = objective_direct(hper)
                if cper < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best_c = cper
                    best_h = hper.copy()
                    best_n = int(hper.size)
            except Exception:
                pass

    best_h = np.asarray(best_h, dtype=float).ravel()
    best_h = np.nan_to_num(best_h, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    best_h = np.clip(best_h, 0.0, 1.0)
    best_h = project_box_sum(best_h, 0.5 * float(best_h.size))

    best_c = objective_direct(best_h)
    best_n = int(best_h.size)

    return best_h, float(best_c), best_n
```
